HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000170/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_066e9e2e9e5978a5b07b","text":"2. **Clarity that also sorts.** **Lexical evidence:** `ٱلْبَيِّنَة` anchors `ب ي ن` B004, “appearance and becoming clear,” and dormantly B001, “separation and parting.” **Structural cue and role:** the same noun closes 98:1 and 98:4, where its arrival is followed by division; nearby 98:3 calls the writings upright. Visibility and separation therefore operate together: the proof supplies a criterion by which responses become distinguishable. **Abductive move:** I infer that what reveals differences can produce a sorting event; the packet gives recurrence and sequence, not this general causal principle. **Reading change:** the proof is no longer static evidence offered to an undifferentiated audience; it is an event that makes latent differences publicly separable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000009/B001","root_000170/B004","root_000170/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_10b01e4ab25c5d61d877","text":"3. **Arrival as a channel crossing into a closed field.** Evidence: ء ت ي B001 arrival, B004 watercourse/clearing flow, B005 torrent from elsewhere; ب ي ن B006 distance/gap and B004 appearance. Structural cue: 98:8’s rivers and flowing imagery retro-activate ء ت ي’s watercourse branch; 98:4’s جاءت البينة repeats arrival. Assigned role: the proof comes from outside the locked social condition like water entering a prepared channel. Abductive move: I infer that an arriving sign can alter the medium it enters, not merely inform observers. Reading change: البينة becomes an incoming event that reconfigures separations and channels, not merely a proposition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001173/B001","root_001173/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2c1d3730ac37f5a6d5ab","text":"1. **The proof as a release point that breaks a held configuration.** **Lexical evidence:** `مُنفَكِّين` anchors `ف ك ك` B003, “separating from something or, under negation, not ceasing,” and B001, “opening what is locked and separating what is interlaced.” **Structural cue and role:** the negated state lasts `حَتَّىٰ` the proof arrives, but 98:4 reverses stillness into `تَفَرَّقَ` only after that proof has come. The branch makes the proof a threshold at which a previously held configuration is unfastened. **Abductive move:** I infer that arrival triggers the release; grammar supplies the boundary and 98:4 supplies the later scattering, but causation is my directional arrow. **Reading change:** `مُنفَكِّين` becomes pressure-loaded rather than merely durative: the group is held until disclosure forces differentiated movement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B001","root_001307/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3f5764f4d10962842e81","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:1:4\",\"root_001307\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İnkâr, giderek daha açık hâle gelen bir bildirimi örtme girişimi olarak keskinleşir ve sonraki ateşte zorunlu açığa çıkışla karşılanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000064/B001","root_001283/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_423b9ea939960957d78b","text":"3. **Textual belonging without enacted fitness.** **Lexical evidence:** `أَهْل` anchors `ء ه ل` B001, “people bound by closeness and belonging,” while B003 supplies “being fit or worthy”; `ٱلْكِتَٰب` anchors `ك ت ب` B001, “joining one thing to another.” **Structural cue and role:** `أَهْل ٱلْكِتَٰب` recurs in 98:4 and 98:6, yet the joined textual object does not stop social scattering or the adverse classification in 98:6. B001 names corporate attachment; B003 functions as a corrected expectation rather than an asserted status. **Abductive move:** I infer that fitness for a received order must be enacted and is not guaranteed by affiliation. **Reading change:** “people of the book” reads as a potentially unstable bond to a joining artifact, not as an automatic commendation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000064/B001","root_001283/B002","root_001332/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_43f17912ada0218df2d4","text":"2. **A document-community bound by record before being judged.** Evidence: ء ه ل B001 people of belonging; ك ت ب B002 written book, B003 binding decree, B004 register/group enrollment; ك و ن B001 temporal predication. Structural cue: 98:2-3 brings purified sheets containing upright books, 98:5 calls the demand دين القيّمة, and 98:6 names the same أهل الكتاب in judgment. Assigned role: “people of the book” are not just holders of text but a group constituted by written obligation. Abductive move: I supply the legal arrow that being enrolled under a book makes the later proof accountable evidence. Reading change: the ayah becomes a status statement about communities already bound by records before the proof arrives.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000009/B004","root_000009/B005","root_000170/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_497a5f328acb4ba80c5c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:1:11\",\"root_000009\",[\"B004\",\"B005\"]],[\"98:1:12\",\"root_000170\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kanıtın gelişi, dışarıdan akan bir suyun kapalı bir alana girip mevcut ayrımları yeniden biçimlendirmesi gibi okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000791/B001","root_000791/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_504790ff7ad535f1b25b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:1:8\",\"root_000791\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ortak koşma, bağlılığın birden çok pay sahibi arasında bölünmesi olarak görünür; sonraki yalnız Allah'a özgü yöneliş bu bölünmeyi giderir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000170/B001","root_000170/B004","root_000791/B001","root_000791/B006","root_001173/B001","root_001307/B001","root_001307/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_88f75eb7051ddd132240","text":"1. **Unbelief as an entangling cover awaiting an unfastener.** Evidence: ك ف ر B001 covering/concealment and B003 covering or rejecting truth; ش ر ك B001 sharing/partnership and B006 hunter’s snare; ف ك ك B001 unfastening, B002 freeing from bondage/snare, B003 non-cessation in negated formulas; ب ي ن B004 clear proof and B001 separation. Structural cue: 98:4 repeats البينة and then reports تفرّق, while 98:6 repeats the same social pair in fire. Assigned role: الكفر covers, الشرك tangles, البينة separates/clarifies, منفكين marks the release that is denied until arrival. Abductive move: I infer that clarification functions like an unlocking force. Reading change: the ayah is less “they would not desist” abstractly and more “they remained fastened into a covered/social snare until a separator arrived.”","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000064/B001","root_000064/B003","root_001283/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9102f2ce7fbbb3484156","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:1:6\",\"root_000064\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]],[\"98:1:7\",\"root_001283\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kitaba mensubiyet, kendiliğinden yeterlilik sağlamaz; alınan birleştirici düzene uygunluk, kanıta verilen karşılıkla kazanılır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b28da5506901552d5da0","text":"98:8’s rivers made the ء ت ي watercourse/torrent branches unexpectedly useful for “تأتيهم البينة.” 98:6 also sharpened منفكين: the same pair who are not released in 98:1 appear as خالدين in 98:6, so non-release can harden into permanence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000791/B003","root_001173/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bf482b842db9a65bf7c6","text":"98:4 unexpectedly reversed the apparent immobility of `مُنفَكِّين`: the proof does not simply end a duration but releases a visible scattering, activating `ف ك ك` B001 beside B003. 98:5 retrospectively decoded fixed `ش ر ك` through the opposite exclusivity of `خ ل ص` B004, and 98:6 sharpened fixed `ك ف ر` B001 and B003 by placing concealment beside fire’s illuminating `ن و ر` branch. Together these later ayat corrected any reading in which clarity mechanically produces consensus.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000064/B001","root_001283/B002","root_001283/B003","root_001283/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c2922f58fd034f9baf94","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:1:6\",\"root_000064\",[\"B001\"]],[\"98:1:7\",\"root_001283\",[\"B002\",\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kitap ehli ifadesi, yalnızca metin sahibi bir topluluğu değil, yazılı ve bağlayıcı bir yükümlülük altında bulunan topluluğu da belirtir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000430/B004","root_000791/B001","root_000791/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d359a7139b2f98b4b7ab","text":"5. **Shared allegiance versus exclusive orientation.** **Lexical evidence:** `ٱلْمُشْرِكِينَ` anchors `ش ر ك` B001, “sharing and partnership,” and B002, “associating a partner with God.” **Structural cue and role:** 98:5 answers this with `مُخْلِصِينَ`, `خ ل ص` B004, “exclusive separation from sharing,” and `حُنَفَاء`, a directional inclination. The fixed term thus names an allocation structure in which devotion is divided among claimants. **Abductive move:** I infer that the later command is a direct repair of the earlier divided allocation; their lexical opposition supplies the basis, while the repair relation is inferred. **Reading change:** `مُشْرِكِينَ` becomes a functional diagnosis of split allegiance, not merely the name of a population.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001307/B001","root_001307/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_efbd3d5140327438eef3","text":"4. **Concealment sharpened into counter-disclosure.** **Lexical evidence:** `كَفَرُوا` anchors `ك ف ر` B001, “covering and concealment,” and B003, “covering or rejecting truth.” **Structural cue and role:** the packet follows the label with a messenger, purified sheets, clear proof, and then repeats the group beside `نَار` in 98:6, whose root `ن و ر` carries illumination as well as fire. The covering branch therefore works against an increasingly visible object. **Abductive move:** I infer deliberate overlay from persistence after disclosure; the packet establishes clarity-before-outcome but does not state the actors’ interior intent. **Reading change:** the fixed verb is sharpened from a generic class label into active resistance to disclosure, later answered by an exposing fire.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000170/B001","root_000170/B004","root_001173/B001","root_001173/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_fcbfb860f72dedf0b999","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"98:1:9\",\"root_001173\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]],[\"98:1:12\",\"root_000170\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Açık kanıt, tutulmuş bir durumun kilidini açan ve gizli ayrımları görünür toplumsal ayrışmaya dönüştüren bir eşik işlevi görür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
